# Target blueprint 00: Control Centre

> **Status.** Archive: proposal, not ratified. A greenfield redesign blueprint written in October 2026. Every area was Draft, Under Review or Revision Required (its own document status) and no test was run against it. Its content was mined into `docs/blueprint/`; `SSOT.md` remains the source of intent. Module federation described here was not adopted: the built system is a modular monolith (`ARCHITECTURE.md`).
>
> **What this file holds.** All files of the former `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/` folder, in order.

## Canonical Target Invariants & Cross-Cutting Rules (INV-T / BR-T seeds)

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/CANONICAL_INVARIANTS.md`._

Doc ID: CC-INV · Owner: Architecture Review Board · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review
All documents cite these IDs; do not restate them differently. Fuller registries: `06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/`.

### Product & data invariants

| ID      | Invariant                                                                                                                                                             | Enforced by                                           | Verified by                                |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| INV-T01 | Every protected endpoint is authorized server-side by `packages/policy`, deny-by-default; client checks are hints only.                                               | policy middleware + route registration lint           | FF-01, authz matrix tests                  |
| INV-T02 | Every record loaded by id is authorized against that record (owner/org/participant/admin); "authenticated" is never sufficient.                                       | policy `can(principal, action, resource)`             | IDOR test suite                            |
| INV-T03 | No cross-tenant read/write: all tenant tables carry `org_id`/`institution_id` and RLS.                                                                                | RLS + repository layer                                | cross-tenant canary tests (FF-05)          |
| INV-T04 | Verified evidence status is set only by server-side verification transitions; a claim is never displayed as verified.                                                 | `evidence` module state machine SM-EVIDENCE           | state-machine tests                        |
| INV-T05 | Verification credentials (email code, referee token) are stored only as hashes, single-use, expiring, bound to one request, never returned to the person they verify. | `verification` module                                 | token tests                                |
| INV-T06 | Assessment AI restrictions (`AI_PROHIBITED`) are enforced in the AI gateway regardless of caller.                                                                     | `api/ai` gateway                                      | gateway test matrix                        |
| INV-T07 | Free-tier users never cause paid inference unless an explicit budget policy flag allows it; every call passes quota.                                                  | AI gateway budget check                               | cost-guard tests                           |
| INV-T08 | AI output and AI "memory" are never evidence or profile truth.                                                                                                        | evidence creation rejects `source=ai`                 | schema test                                |
| INV-T09 | No autonomous consequential decision about a person (reject, rank-out, flag, ban) — a human acts or confirms.                                                         | workflow design; no auto-transition on AI/score alone | review checklist + tests                   |
| INV-T10 | Every consequential side effect is idempotent (idempotency key or natural key) and audited.                                                                           | `Idempotency-Key` middleware, audit port              | replay tests                               |
| INV-T11 | State changes follow declared state machines only; terminal states are immutable.                                                                                     | domain state-machine tables                           | SM tests                                   |
| INV-T12 | Events are written to the outbox in the same transaction as the state change (no dual write).                                                                         | repository `withOutbox`                               | transaction tests                          |
| INV-T13 | Offline caches are never authoritative; queued writes are revalidated server-side.                                                                                    | API + client sync layer                               | offline conflict tests                     |
| INV-T14 | Secrets never reach the browser or logs; PII redacted in logs.                                                                                                        | config/observability                                  | secret-scan, log tests                     |
| INV-T15 | Consent is purpose-bound, versioned, revocable; recruiter access to evidence requires a live consent scope.                                                           | `privacy` module                                      | consent tests                              |
| INV-T16 | Remotes never read credentials/tokens and never import another remote's internals.                                                                                    | host contract + lint                                  | FF-03, FF-04                               |
| INV-T17 | Payment redirect success grants nothing; only verified webhook state changes entitlements.                                                                            | billing module                                        | webhook tests                              |
| INV-T18 | Privileged (support/admin) access to personal data is time-boxed, reason-coded and audited.                                                                           | admin module                                          | audit tests                                |
| INV-T19 | Matching/ranking is explainable, shows uncertainty, and never uses payment as a ranking input.                                                                        | matching module                                       | explanation tests, ranking-input allowlist |
| INV-T20 | Anomaly ≠ guilt: integrity flags route to human review and are appealable.                                                                                            | assessment/safety                                     | process tests                              |

### Architecture fitness functions (automatable)

| ID    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Tooling                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| FF-01 | Every route declares a policy action; routes without one fail CI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | route-registry lint                                                                                                                                                 |
| FF-02 | No import across `api/<module>` except via its `index.ts` public interface.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | dependency-cruiser                                                                                                                                                  |
| FF-03 | No remote imports another remote's source; only shell-provided contracts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | dependency-cruiser + MF config check                                                                                                                                |
| FF-04 | Federation dependency graph is acyclic; shared deps have a singleton + version range.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | manifest validator                                                                                                                                                  |
| FF-05 | Cross-tenant canary: seeded tenant B data must be unreadable as tenant A in every list/get endpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | generated integration suite                                                                                                                                         |
| FF-06 | Every public contract change passes OpenAPI diff (breaking = major version).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | oasdiff                                                                                                                                                             |
| FF-07 | Every unsafe mutation route accepts `Idempotency-Key`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | route-registry lint                                                                                                                                                 |
| FF-08 | Remote release requires compatibility check vs current shell + API ranges.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | CI compat gate                                                                                                                                                      |
| FF-09 | Every PWA capability used has a feature-detected fallback listed in the platform matrix.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | review + e2e on WebKit                                                                                                                                              |
| FF-10 | Migrations are append-only, contiguous, and each has a rollback/forward-fix note.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | migration lint                                                                                                                                                      |
| FF-11 | State vocabulary has one source: `06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/STATE_MACHINES.md` (SM-00). Every DB `CHECK (state/status/lifecycle IN (...))`, OpenAPI state enum (schemas and query filters), AsyncAPI state-bearing enum and UI status lookup equals the persisted SM-00 state set of its machine; any difference fails CI ("enum-vs-SM lint").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | enum-vs-SM lint (machine-readable SM table in `packages/domain`; generates the others)                                                                              |
| FF-12 | Consent scope vocabulary has one source: `00_CONTROL_CENTRE/CONSENT_SCOPE_CATALOGUE.md` section 8 (CSC). The DB `consent_record.scope` CHECK and `consent_grain_ck`, the OpenAPI `ConsentScope` enum (referenced by every consent schema and the `{scope}` path parameter), the DT-01 scope list, the PRIVACY purposes register and revocation table, and every `require_consent(` call use exactly the catalogue ids; no `CS-*` token or retired `evidence.share_recruiter` appears outside the CSC crosswalk, explicit "was `CS-...`" / "retired" annotations and review reports. Any difference fails CI. | consent-scope lint (generates the CHECK, enum and DT-01 header from the CSC machine-readable block; documentation lint over this folder until the generator exists) |

### Cross-cutting business rules (seed set; full registry in 06_*)

BR-T001 one active application per (candidate, job). BR-T002 application stage transitions only per SM-APPLICATION. BR-T003 evidence artefacts virus-scanned before becoming visible. BR-T004 reference requests expire after 14 days; max 2 reminders. BR-T005 revoked evidence triggers recomputation of dependent skill confidence and match explanations (async, audited). BR-T041 privileged access grant ≤ 4 h, reason required. BR-T050 session refresh-token reuse revokes the whole token family. BR-T060 erasure completes within 30 days except legally retained audit/billing records (retention list OPEN-08).

## Consent Scope Catalogue (CSC)

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/CONSENT_SCOPE_CATALOGUE.md`._

Doc ID: CC-CSC · Owner: Privacy Lead + Data Architect (co-owner: Product Director) · Version 1.0 · Status: Under Review · Date: 2026-10-25
Resolves: REVW-02 (Critical, two consent vocabularies); closes the timing half of REVW-25 and the consent-end half of REVW-26 (open parts in section 9).
Supersedes: nothing as a document. It consolidates, and is now the only authority for, the scope ids formerly written as `CS-*` in SPEC-G1 section 3.2, DT-01, SPEC-G2, SPEC-G5 and FEAT-22-01.4, and the dotted vocabulary in DB-01 section 4 / DATA-E2201. Those places keep their prose but must use the ids below.
Linked from: [MASTER_INDEX](#master-index) · [ID_REGISTRY](#id-registry-canonical--all-documents-must-use-these-ids). Fitness function: FF-12 in [CANONICAL_INVARIANTS](#canonical-target-invariants--cross-cutting-rules-inv-t--br-t-seeds).
Classification: all content is **[TARGET]** unless tagged **[OPEN]**. Nothing here is implemented. Store all generated artifacts under: Yash_Final_Project_Document.

### 1. Canonical id format (decision)

`<area>.<purpose>`: two lowercase segments, `snake_case` within a segment, regex `^[a-z]+\.[a-z_]+(\.[a-z_]+)?$`. The optional third segment exists for one id only, `ai.assist.process`, kept unchanged because the DB, BE-01 AI gateway flow and privacy design already use it; new scopes use two segments. Dotted ids are chosen because they already exist in the DB CHECK, the OpenAPI enum, the policy action registry style (`<module>.<resource>.<verb>`) and the consent API path (`/me/consents/{scope}`).

| Rule                  | Detail                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Areas (closed list)   | `profile`, `search`, `matching`, `outreach`, `evidence`, `application`, `alumni`, `ai`, `analytics`, `comms`, `data`                     |
| Immutable             | An id is never renamed or reused. A scope whose meaning changes materially gets a new id, and the old id moves to section 4 as `retired` |
| Description change    | Same id, new `policy_version`; the scope is treated as not granted until re-confirmed (privacy design section 3, re-consent)             |
| Legacy `CS-*`         | Retired as identifiers. They survive only in the crosswalk (section 4). Documents must not introduce new `CS-*` ids                      |
| One purpose per scope | Never bundled with terms acceptance, never pre-ticked (privacy design section 3)                                                         |
| Source of truth       | The machine-readable block in section 8. Every other list of scope ids is derived from it (FF-12)                                        |

### 2. Grain model

A grant is one append-only `consent_record` row (INV-T35). Three independent dimensions describe it. This replaces the earlier overloaded `target_type` that meant both "the object" and "the grantee".

| Column                        | Meaning                                              | Values                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `scope`                       | What purpose is consented                            | section 3 ids                                                                                           |
| `target_type` / `target_id`   | The **data object** the grant is about               | `evidence`, `application`, or NULL (whole account)                                                      |
| `grantee_type` / `grantee_id` | **Who** may use it                                   | `all_verified_orgs` (no id), `org` (org id), `institution` (institution id), or NULL (no named grantee) |
| `field_set`                   | Field names the learner chose to share (alumni only) | text[] or NULL                                                                                          |
| `expires_at`                  | End of the grant, or NULL for "until revoked"        | timestamptz                                                                                             |

Grain classes (a generated table CHECK `consent_grain_ck` enforces the class per scope, section 5):

| Grain            | grantee                             | target                                                              | Used by                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `account`        | NULL                                | NULL                                                                | `profile.public_view`, `matching.recommend`, `ai.assist.process`, `analytics.product`, `comms.marketing_email`, `data.research_aggregate` |
| `audience`       | `all_verified_orgs`                 | NULL                                                                | `search.discoverable`, `outreach.recruiter_contact`, `evidence.share_summary`                                                             |
| `grantee`        | `org` or `institution`, id required | NULL                                                                | `profile.share_contact`, `evidence.share_institution`, `alumni.share_outcomes`                                                            |
| `grantee_object` | `org`, id required                  | `evidence` optional (NULL = all my evidence artefacts at read time) | `evidence.share_artefact`                                                                                                                 |
| `application`    | `org` (the job's org), id required  | `application`, id required                                          | `application.share_snapshot`                                                                                                              |

No separate grant table is needed: per-employer and per-artefact grants are rows of the same ledger, folded by the `consent_current` view (section 5). One source of truth, as SPEC-G1 section 3.3 rule 1 requires.

### 3. Catalogue

#### 3.1 Identity, grain and defaults

| Scope id                     | Legacy id(s)                                                             | Purpose (user-facing text key, versioned)                                 | PUR    | Target              | Grantee           | Grain          | Default           | User toggle                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------------- | ----------------- | -------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------- |
| `profile.public_view`        | DB: same                                                                 | "Make my profile visible to anyone with its public page"                  | PUR-02 | none                | none (anyone)     | account        | off               | yes                                     |
| `search.discoverable`        | `CS-DISCOVER`                                                            | "Let recruiters find my profile summary in search"                        | PUR-21 | none                | all verified orgs | audience       | off               | yes                                     |
| `matching.recommend`         | DB: same                                                                 | "Use my profile and evidence to recommend jobs and explain matches to me" | PUR-06 | none                | none              | account        | off               | yes                                     |
| `outreach.recruiter_contact` | `CS-CONTACT`; DB: same                                                   | "Let recruiters send me first-contact requests"                           | PUR-07 | none                | all verified orgs | audience       | off               | yes                                     |
| `evidence.share_summary`     | `CS-EVID-SUMMARY`                                                        | "Show evidence titles, skills and strength to recruiters who find me"     | PUR-22 | none                | all verified orgs | audience       | off               | yes                                     |
| `evidence.share_artefact`    | `CS-EVID-ARTEFACT`; DB: `evidence.share_recruiter` (org/evidence target) | "Let this employer open my evidence files"                                | PUR-22 | evidence (optional) | one org           | grantee_object | off               | yes                                     |
| `profile.share_contact`      | `CS-CONTACT-DETAILS`                                                     | "Share my email/phone with this employer"                                 | PUR-22 | none                | one org           | grantee        | off               | yes                                     |
| `application.share_snapshot` | `CS-APPLICATION`; DB: `evidence.share_recruiter` (application target)    | Implicit: "This application shows exactly these items to this employer"   | PUR-05 | application         | the job's org     | application    | granted at submit | **no** (withdraw per application)       |
| `evidence.share_institution` | DB: same                                                                 | "Include my work in my institution's cohort reports"                      | PUR-18 | none                | one institution   | grantee        | off               | yes                                     |
| `alumni.share_outcomes`      | `CS-ALUMNI`                                                              | "Share chosen outcomes with my institution after I graduate"              | PUR-23 | none                | one institution   | grantee        | off               | yes, offered at graduation (FEAT-18-07) |
| `ai.assist.process`          | DB: same                                                                 | "Send my content, redacted, to AI providers to get suggestions"           | PUR-10 | none                | none (processor)  | account        | off               | yes                                     |
| `analytics.product`          | DB: same                                                                 | "Help improve the product with usage analytics"                           | PUR-14 | none                | none              | account        | off               | yes                                     |
| `comms.marketing_email`      | DB: same                                                                 | "Send me tips and product news by email"                                  | PUR-13 | none                | none              | account        | off               | yes                                     |
| `data.research_aggregate`    | DB: same                                                                 | "Include my data in anonymised research"                                  | PUR-20 | none                | none              | account        | off               | **hidden until OPEN-B10 is decided**    |

#### 3.2 Expiry, revocation effect and links

"Immediate" = enforced at read time (section 6). Fuller wording and the "cannot be undone" column live in PRIVACY_AND_DATA_LIFECYCLE_DESIGN section 3, keyed by these ids.

| Scope id                     | Expiry                                                                                                                                                        | Revocation effect                                                                                                                                                   | Linked rules                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `profile.public_view`        | none; re-confirm prompt every {12 months} is not an expiry                                                                                                    | Public page 404; caches purged; search docs removed                                                                                                                 | DT-01 row 2; INV-T15                                                                                            |
| `search.discoverable`        | none; re-confirm prompt every {12 months} (SPEC-G1 section 5)                                                                                                 | Absent from recruiter results at next read; index entry removed <= 15 min (BR-T063)                                                                                 | DT-01 row 5; BR-T063, BR-T069 (restriction is a profile setting, not a scope); INV-T15, INV-T29; AC-22-01-01/02 |
| `matching.recommend`         | none                                                                                                                                                          | No new recommendations; `match_explanation`, embeddings deleted (consent_epoch bump); candidate stays lexically searchable if `search.discoverable` is live (DEP-2) | INV-T15, INV-T19                                                                                                |
| `outreach.recruiter_contact` | none                                                                                                                                                          | New first-contact requests denied; open threads remain but recruiter cannot continue                                                                                | DT-08 rows 3 and 6; AC-13-02-01/02; INV-T40 blocklist is independent                                            |
| `evidence.share_summary`     | none                                                                                                                                                          | Summary fields dropped from results at next read; effective only while `search.discoverable` is live (DEP-1)                                                        | DT-01 row 5; INV-T15, INV-T29                                                                                   |
| `evidence.share_artefact`    | optional; default {12 months}; reminder 14 d before; ends automatically if the org is deleted                                                                 | That org's reads and new signed-URL issuance denied; URLs already issued lapse within their <= 5 min life (BE-01)                                                   | DT-01 row 6; FEAT-22-01.4; AC-22-01-03; INV-T15, INV-T29; BR-T063                                               |
| `profile.share_contact`      | optional; default {12 months} **[OPEN-CS-05]** (SPEC-G1 stated none)                                                                                          | Email/phone masked for that org at next read; a contact path that exists through an accepted conversation (DT-08) is separate and is not removed by this revocation | DT-01 row 7; INV-T15                                                                                            |
| `application.share_snapshot` | `expires_at` = terminal state time + {12 months} **[OPEN-08]**, written by the applications module on the terminal transition; candidate may withdraw earlier | Snapshot kept; live items become tombstones; org-side identifiable data handled by rule R-RET (section 7)                                                           | DT-01 row 4; BR-T062, BR-T063; INV-T29, INV-T35; PUR-05                                                         |
| `evidence.share_institution` | ends with the cohort sunset (graduation date + 24 h, SPEC-G5 section 3.7)                                                                                     | Learner excluded from cohort reads and aggregates; aggregates recomputed                                                                                            | DT-01 row 10; BR-T100, BR-T105; INV-T41; **[OPEN-CS-06]**                                                       |
| `alumni.share_outcomes`      | none (until revoked)                                                                                                                                          | Institution loses the chosen fields at next read                                                                                                                    | DT-01 row 10a; FEAT-18-07; AC-18-07-04; INV-T41                                                                 |
| `ai.assist.process`          | none                                                                                                                                                          | AI endpoints return `403 consent-required`; queued AI jobs cancelled (R5)                                                                                           | BE-01 AI gateway section 13; INV-T06..T09; PUR-10                                                               |
| `analytics.product`          | none                                                                                                                                                          | Client stops sending; server rejects; subject key discarded                                                                                                         | PUR-14; REVW-31 for the separate basis of operational telemetry                                                 |
| `comms.marketing_email`      | none                                                                                                                                                          | Marketing sends suppressed at send time; provider list synced (R6)                                                                                                  | DT-07 row 8; INTEGRATION_ARCHITECTURE email categories                                                          |
| `data.research_aggregate`    | none                                                                                                                                                          | Subject excluded from the next aggregate run                                                                                                                        | PUR-20; OPEN-B10                                                                                                |

### 4. Crosswalk (old to canonical)

Every id that appears or appeared in the specs, DT-01, the DB, the API, BE-01 or the privacy design maps to exactly one row, except `evidence.share_recruiter`, which is split by the target it carried.

| Old id                                                                                                                                                                          | Where it was used                                                                                          | Canonical id                                                                   | Note                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CS-DISCOVER`                                                                                                                                                                   | SPEC-G1 3.2, 5, AC-22-01-01/02; DT-01 rows 5; SPEC-G5 AC-11-05-01; SPEC-G1 field visibility `discoverable` | `search.discoverable`                                                          | New to the DB list                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CS-CONTACT`                                                                                                                                                                    | SPEC-G1 3.2; SPEC-G5 3 and AC-13-02-01/02; DT-08                                                           | `outreach.recruiter_contact`                                                   | Existing DB id                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CS-EVID-SUMMARY`                                                                                                                                                               | SPEC-G1 3.2; SPEC-G2 section 3.2 rule 10; DT-01 row 5                                                      | `evidence.share_summary`                                                       | New to the DB list                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CS-EVID-ARTEFACT`                                                                                                                                                              | SPEC-G1 3.2; SPEC-G2; FEAT-22-01.4; DT-01 row 6; AC-22-01-03                                               | `evidence.share_artefact`                                                      | New to the DB list; per-employer by `grantee_id`                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CS-CONTACT-DETAILS`                                                                                                                                                            | SPEC-G1 3.2; DT-01 row 7                                                                                   | `profile.share_contact`                                                        | New to the DB list                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CS-ALUMNI`                                                                                                                                                                     | SPEC-G1 3.2; SPEC-G5 3.7, AC-18-07-04                                                                      | `alumni.share_outcomes`                                                        | New to the DB list; uses `field_set`                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CS-APPLICATION`                                                                                                                                                                | SPEC-G1 3.2; DT-01 header; BR-T062                                                                         | `application.share_snapshot`                                                   | Written as a ledger row (`source='application'`), not a toggle                                                                                                                                    |
| `evidence.share_recruiter` with `target_type=application`                                                                                                                       | DB-01 section 4; PUR-05; RLS P5; require_consent in BE-01                                                  | `application.share_snapshot`                                                   | Retired id                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `evidence.share_recruiter` with `target_type` `org` or `evidence`                                                                                                               | DB-01 section 4; RLS P5; BE-01                                                                             | `evidence.share_artefact`                                                      | Retired id. An old `evidence`-only target with no grantee has no canonical equivalent: a grant to "any recruiter" is no longer expressible. No data exists (all [TARGET]), so nothing is migrated |
| `evidence.share_recruiter` used for search-result summaries                                                                                                                     | implicit in `matching.recommend` text                                                                      | `evidence.share_summary`                                                       | Split out                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `matching.recommend` as "search presence"                                                                                                                                       | DB-01 section 4 purpose text                                                                               | `search.discoverable` (presence) + `matching.recommend` (recommendations only) | `matching.recommend` is narrowed; its id is unchanged                                                                                                                                             |
| `profile.public_view`, `evidence.share_institution`, `outreach.recruiter_contact`, `ai.assist.process`, `analytics.product`, `comms.marketing_email`, `data.research_aggregate` | DB, API, privacy design                                                                                    | same id                                                                        | Unchanged                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Transition: every source is [TARGET], so there is no data to convert. The only imported consent is `profile.public_view` with `source='import'` (REFERENCE_TO_TARGET_DATA_MIGRATION). The retired `evidence.share_recruiter` is rejected by the CHECK and the OpenAPI enum from the first migration.

### 5. Record shape and generated artefacts

`consent_record` columns added or changed (DATA-E2201): `target_type` CHECK in (`evidence`, `application`); new `grantee_type` CHECK in (`all_verified_orgs`, `org`, `institution`); new `grantee_id` uuid; new `field_set` text[] (only `alumni.share_outcomes`); new `expires_at` timestamptz.

`consent_current` = latest row per `(subject_account_id, scope, target_type, target_id, grantee_type, grantee_id)`, exposed only where `granted AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`. Expiry therefore needs no write at expiry time; the sweeper only notifies and bumps `consent_epoch`.

Generated table CHECK (template; the per-scope branches are produced from the section 8 block):

```sql
CONSTRAINT consent_grain_ck CHECK (
  CASE scope
    WHEN 'search.discoverable'        THEN grantee_type = 'all_verified_orgs' AND target_type IS NULL
    WHEN 'evidence.share_artefact'    THEN grantee_type = 'org' AND grantee_id IS NOT NULL AND (target_type IS NULL OR target_type = 'evidence')
    WHEN 'application.share_snapshot' THEN grantee_type = 'org' AND grantee_id IS NOT NULL AND target_type = 'application' AND target_id IS NOT NULL
    WHEN 'profile.public_view'        THEN grantee_type IS NULL AND target_type IS NULL
    -- ... one branch per scope, from the catalogue
  END)
```

`require_consent` obligation (BE-01): `require_consent(scope, { grantee, target })`. Examples that now resolve through `ConsentPort`:

| Read                                                        | Obligation                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Recruiter opens a search result summary                     | `require_consent('search.discoverable', {grantee: {org: orgId}})` and `require_consent('evidence.share_summary', {grantee: {org: orgId}})`   |
| Recruiter opens an evidence artefact outside an application | `require_consent('evidence.share_artefact', {grantee: {org: orgId}, target: {evidence: id}})`                                                |
| Hiring team opens evidence inside an application            | `require_consent('application.share_snapshot', {grantee: {org: orgId}, target: {application: id}})`, plus the live-item check in DT-01 row 4 |
| Recruiter requests contact details                          | `require_consent('profile.share_contact', {grantee: {org: orgId}})` or an accepted conversation (DT-08)                                      |
| AI gateway call                                             | `require_consent('ai.assist.process')`                                                                                                       |

### 6. Revocation propagation (REVW-25)

Decision: **authorization at read time against `consent_current` on the primary database, with no consent cache.** The four earlier statements (instantly, within 1 minute, within a cache TTL, next API read) collapse to one: a revocation is effective for every read that starts after the revoking transaction commits. 60 s is kept only as the **test and monitoring timeout**, not as a tolerated stale window. Tags: the design is [TARGET]; every number marked [OPEN] has no measured evidence yet and is a placeholder bound to be confirmed by the named owner.

| Path                                                                      | Mechanism                                                                                                                                   | Bound                                                          | Status                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| R1 API and RLS reads (policy `can` plus `ConsentPort`, RLS P5/P5b)        | Read-time lookup on the primary; consent reads never go to a replica                                                                        | Effective on the first read after commit; test timeout 60 s    | [TARGET]; replica-lag rule needs Backend Architect sign-off |
| R1-fallback                                                               | Only if profiling shows the lookup is too costly: a per-account decision cache keyed by `consent_epoch`                                     | TTL <= 60 s, and any `consent_epoch` change invalidates it     | [OPEN-CS-01]                                                |
| R2 Search results                                                         | Query-time check that the candidate document's `consent_epoch` equals the account's current epoch, so a stale index entry is never returned | 0 s for visibility; physical index removal <= 15 min (BR-T063) | [TARGET]                                                    |
| R3 Signed download URLs already issued                                    | Natural expiry                                                                                                                              | <= 5 min (BE-01 download row)                                  | [TARGET]                                                    |
| R4 Derived data (embeddings, `match_explanation`, `search_candidate_doc`) | Event-driven deletion after `consent.changed`                                                                                               | <= 24 h                                                        | [OPEN-CS-02]                                                |
| R5 Queued AI jobs                                                         | Worker cancels jobs for the subject                                                                                                         | <= 5 min                                                       | [OPEN-CS-02]                                                |
| R6 Marketing provider list sync                                           | Sends re-check consent at send time (0 s); provider suppression list sync                                                                   | <= 24 h                                                        | [OPEN-CS-02]                                                |
| R7 Analytics clients                                                      | Server rejects at once; client reads the new state on next config fetch                                                                     | next session start                                             | [OPEN-CS-02]                                                |
| R8 `consent.changed` consumers                                            | Outbox, at-least-once, idempotent consumers                                                                                                 | p95 <= 60 s                                                    | [OPEN-CS-02]                                                |
| R9 Access-log entry                                                       | Written in the revoking transaction (BR-T063)                                                                                               | same transaction                                               | [TARGET]                                                    |
| R10 Recruiter-device offline copies                                       | Not revocable remotely; cache is non-authoritative (INV-T13)                                                                                | bounded by PWA cache lifetime                                  | [OPEN-CS-02] with 08_PWA                                    |

Test alignment: TEST-SEC-009 asserts denial on the first read after the revoke response (not "within the cache TTL"). INV-T25's 60 s cache bound concerns the verified rung, not consent.

### 7. Rules

| ID              | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEP-1           | `evidence.share_summary` has no effect unless `search.discoverable` is live for the same viewer org (DT-01 row 5).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| DEP-2           | The recruiter search document is built lexically when `search.discoverable` is live. Embeddings and semantic ranking for that candidate require `matching.recommend` to be live as well. [OPEN-CS-03] confirm with the Search Architect.                                                                                                          |
| DEP-3           | `evidence.share_artefact` and `profile.share_contact` are independent of `search.discoverable`: a candidate may grant an employer they applied to without being searchable.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| DEP-4           | Org blocklist (INV-T40) and `BR-T069` restriction are checked in policy in addition to consent and are never represented as scopes.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| R-APP           | `application.share_snapshot` is written in the same transaction as the application and its `application_consent_snapshot.consent_record_ids`; the apply screen must show the exact items and org (privacy design section 3).                                                                                                                      |
| R-RET (REVW-26) | When `application.share_snapshot` expires, the org's identifiable copy of the candidate record is pseudonymised unless the org holds a documented basis. DATA-021's "platform max 24 months" then applies to the pseudonymised record only. Basis and wording to be set with counsel under OPEN-08; DATA-021 itself is not edited by this change. |
| R-EXP           | Expiry sweeper notifies 14 d before expiry (SPEC-G1 section 5), then bumps `consent_epoch` once at expiry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| R-VER           | Each grant stores `policy_version`. A version bump of a scope's text makes the earlier grant not live until re-confirmed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### 10. Generation and lint rule (FF-12)

FF-12 (in CANONICAL_INVARIANTS) fails CI when any of the following disagree with the section 8 block: (1) the DB `consent_record.scope` CHECK and `consent_grain_ck`; (2) the OpenAPI `ConsentScope` enum (single component, referenced by `ConsentState`, `ConsentRecord`, `ConsentGrantInput` and the `{scope}` path parameter); (3) the scope list in the DT-01 header; (4) the scope column of the PRIVACY processing-purposes register and the revocation table; (5) every backticked id matching the section 1 regex with an area in section 1 that appears in a `require_consent(` call; (6) any remaining legacy `CS-*` token or the retired `evidence.share_recruiter` outside this catalogue's crosswalk, explicit "was `CS-...`" / "retired" annotations and the review reports. Until the generator exists, the same check runs as a documentation lint over this folder.

### Status vocabulary

- **Discovery:** evidence and scope are still being gathered.
- **Draft:** content exists but has not completed substantive review.
- **Under Review:** content has a defined owner and is being checked.
- **Revision Required:** a review found material gaps or contradictions.
- **Blocked:** progress depends on an external decision, environment, or evidence.
- **Ready for Implementation:** critical behavior and contracts are sufficiently explicit for an implementation work item; this is not production readiness.
- **Deferred:** intentionally outside the current milestone.

### Verification notes

- The current blueprint is a target design. It does not document implementation verification.
- Review counts and finding dispositions are maintained in `27_INDEPENDENT_REVIEWS/FINAL_REVIEW_REPORT.md`.
- Device behavior, legal interpretation, production controls, and stakeholder approval require evidence outside this documentation review.

### Patch round 2026-10-27: evidence labels, reconciliations and triage

Evidence label vocabulary (applies to every statement in this folder): **SOURCE** (quoted from a repository source, dated), **VERIFIED** (confirmed by a command run in this round and named), **TARGET_APPROVED** (an owner or the ARB approved it; no document has this label yet), **PROPOSED** (written in the target design, not ratified), **OPEN** (unresolved). Default for any design statement without a label is PROPOSED. Nothing is labelled implemented, tested or released: `tests_executed = 0`, no build or validator was run in this round.

Doc ID: CC-LED2 · Additive; the original [EXECUTION_LEDGER](#execution-ledger) is left as is (V9 forbids overwriting existing files in this root).

<a id="record-execution-ledger-v9-addendum--honest-disclosure-about-preservation"></a>

### Honest disclosure about preservation

The V8 phase of this work edited files **in place** inside this folder: the orchestrator's own earlier files and, through delegated Sonnet agents, many agent-written files (consent catalogue, state-machine vocabulary, cookie contract, audit/erasure design, permission-matrix rows, ledger append, status lines in the blueprint and readiness docs). These edits were made before the V9 rule ("existing files inside the root must not be destructively changed") was given. Git history holds the prior versions where those files were committed (the folder was committed by another session; check `git log -- Yash_Final_Project_Document`). From this point the work is **additive only** in this root: new revision/addendum files, no in-place edits.

The manifest and baseline were produced by a parallel effort and are reused, not duplicated.
- Review findings: REVW-01..05 resolved in docs. REVW-06..45: four fix agents (frontend/PWA/federation, product/rules/roadmap, backend/data/API/security, ops/QA/cost) were interrupted twice by usage limits (reset 6pm America/Los_Angeles); partial edits exist, completion unverified. - Model policy: Sonnet only, via the Agent tool `model` parameter; effort used: high/xhigh. Not independently verified beyond that parameter. Haiku not used.

## Project Scope & Execution Boundary

Doc ID: CC-SCOPE · Version 1.0 · Status: Final for this initiative

**Mission:** design and document the strongest practical target version of TalentSphere from first principles, as an implementation-ready blueprint.

**Allowed:** read repo files for context; research; write documentation under `Yash_Final_Project_Document/` only.
**Not done / not allowed:** application source changes, migrations, deployments, infra, secrets, pushes/merges, commits (no commit made).
**Model policy:** orchestrator = Claude Sonnet 5.5; all delegated agents invoked with `model: sonnet`. Haiku was not used. Runtime enforcement of the model setting is by the Agent tool parameter; it was not independently verified beyond that parameter.
**Authority of inputs:** existing `SSOT.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, and `docs/` are preserved **[REF]** evidence, not automatically adopted target requirements. The blueprint is a proposed target; source-to-target differences remain unresolved until recorded with owner disposition. Target decisions live in `24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS/ADR_REGISTER.md` and open owner decisions in `OPEN_DECISIONS.md`.
**Reality check:** this is a design; no feature is claimed implemented, and documentation completeness does not prove production readiness.
**Honest limits of this initiative:** no real stakeholder research; platform (iOS/Android) behaviour from secondary sources, unverified on devices; no legal review; no pricing, cost or schedule data; Module Federation + Vite plugin stability needs a spike (OPEN-11).
<!-- source-record-end: Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/PROJECT_SCOPE.md -->

## Glossary

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/GLOSSARY.md`._

| Term                               | Meaning                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Evidence                           | Artifact or observation supporting a skill claim, with provenance, recency, visibility and a rung on the evidence ladder.                                             |
| Evidence provenance                | Attributable origin and validation context for evidence, including source reliability, directness, recency, lifecycle, visibility, conflicts, and downstream effects. |
| Evidence ladder                    | `claimed → artifact-backed → peer-attested → authority-verified` (ADR-T14).                                                                                           |
| Verification                       | Auditable server-side act moving evidence/work-history up the ladder.                                                                                                 |
| Referee                            | Non-account person who attests work history via a single-use tokenised link (INV-T05).                                                                                |
| Credential                         | Formal attestation by an issuer (course, institution, assessment) with lifecycle (issued/suspended/revoked).                                                          |
| Capability                         | Evidence-informed representation of what a person can perform in a defined context; not a guarantee of future performance.                                            |
| Goal                               | A user-defined career or development objective with a desired outcome and relevant target capabilities.                                                               |
| Experience                         | An attributable period or body of work that may support claims and evidence.                                                                                          |
| Opportunity                        | A career-related option, such as a job, project, apprenticeship, scholarship, volunteer placement, or mentorship.                                                     |
| Assessment                         | Governed evaluation with tasks, policy, attempts, scoring, and integrity controls.                                                                                    |
| Learning resource / path           | A course, lesson, activity, or ordered program that supports a defined learning outcome.                                                                              |
| Recommendation                     | Explainable suggested action, content, or opportunity with evidence, uncertainty, and freshness context.                                                              |
| Contribution                       | Attributable work or community activity that may create evidence; activity alone does not establish skill.                                                            |
| Entitlement                        | Access state derived from subscription, policy, or organization licensing.                                                                                            |
| Audit event                        | Attributable record of a consequential action and its relevant context.                                                                                               |
| Trust signal                       | Bounded input used to assess integrity or confidence; it is not proof of misconduct on its own.                                                                       |
| Consent scope                      | Purpose-bound, versioned, revocable permission that gates recruiter/institution reads.                                                                                |
| Host shell                         | Module Federation host app: auth session, router, design system, remote loader.                                                                                       |
| Remote                             | Independently built/deployed frontend module (MOD-CAND…MOD-ADM).                                                                                                      |
| Host Contract                      | Versioned TypeScript/semver interface the shell exposes to remotes (ADR-T18).                                                                                         |
| Remote manifest                    | Signed JSON describing a remote build: version, compat ranges, assets, SRI.                                                                                           |
| Outbox                             | Table written in the same transaction as state change; worker publishes events (ADR-T09).                                                                             |
| Fenced lease                       | Job claim carrying a generation token so stale workers cannot complete jobs.                                                                                          |
| Capability endpoint                | `GET /me/capabilities`; UI hints only.                                                                                                                                |
| Sandbox runner                     | Isolated, network-less service that executes candidate code for assessments (ADR-T13).                                                                                |
| BFF                                | Term for the same-origin session routes (`/api/v1/auth/*`, `GET /me`) that set httpOnly cookies so JS never sees tokens. There is no separate `/bff` URL namespace.   |
| Explainable match                  | Match result with shown factors, evidence references and uncertainty (ADR-T22).                                                                                       |
| Tenant                             | Organization or institution; isolation unit (ADR-T21).                                                                                                                |
| Fitness function (FF)              | Automatable architecture rule checked in CI.                                                                                                                          |
| [REF] / [CONF] / [TARGET] / [OPEN] | Reference / confirmed / target decision / open assumption.                                                                                                            |

Skills and evidence semantics must remain versioned and attributable. A conflicting authoritative record is surfaced as disputed; revocation or correction preserves history and triggers downstream impact review rather than silently rewriting a prior claim (root `SSOT.md` §§6.1–6.3; target lifecycle contracts remain in 06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES).

### Frontend modules (MOD)

| ID        | Module             | Kind   | Route prefix                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------- | ------------------ | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| MOD-SHELL | Host shell         | host   | `/`, `/auth`, `/login`, `/signup`, `/workspaces`, `/help`, `/legal`, `/offline`, `/error`, `/invite`, `/c` (public credential), `/p` (public profile), `/r` (referee request); source: SCREEN_REGISTRY SCR-SHELL-* (REVW-35) |
| MOD-CAND  | `mf-candidate`     | remote | `/me/*` (profile, portfolio, evidence, resumes, career)                                                                                                                                                                      |
| MOD-LRN   | `mf-learning`      | remote | `/learn/*` (courses, assessments, challenges, instructor studio)                                                                                                                                                             |
| MOD-OPP   | `mf-opportunities` | remote | `/jobs/*`, `/applications/*`                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| MOD-HIR   | `mf-hiring`        | remote | `/hiring/*`                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| MOD-MSG   | `mf-messaging`     | remote | `/messages/*`, `/network/*`, `/notifications`                                                                                                                                                                                |
| MOD-ACC   | `mf-account`       | remote | `/settings/*`, `/billing/*`, `/privacy/*`                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| MOD-ADM   | `mf-admin`         | remote | `/admin/*`, `/institution/*`                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

Backend modules (BMOD) use the `api/<name>` names in the table above, plus `apps/worker` (BMOD-WORKER) and `packages/policy` (BMOD-POLICY).

### Roles (ROLE)

ROLE-CAND candidate/learner · ROLE-INST instructor · ROLE-REC recruiter · ROLE-HM hiring manager · ROLE-ORGADM org admin · ROLE-INSTADM institution admin · ROLE-FAC faculty · ROLE-REFEREE referee (tokenised, non-account) · ROLE-MENTOR mentor · ROLE-MOD moderator · ROLE-SUP support operator · ROLE-ADM platform admin · ROLE-AUD auditor (read-only) · ROLE-SVC service/integration account · ROLE-ANON anonymous.

### Identifier prefixes

GOAL, DOM, CAP, MOD, FEAT (`FEAT-<DOM>-NN`), FR, NFR, RULE (`BR-T###`), INV (`INV-T##`), UJ, UI (screens `SCR-*`), ACT (`ACT-*`), API (`API-*`), EVT (`EVT-*`), DATA (`DATA-*`), AC, TEST, ADR (`ADR-T01…`; ADR-T23 is proposed and pending ratification), RISK (`RISK-NN`), OPEN (`OPEN-NN`), SM (state machine `SM-*`).

Root `SSOT.md` `F-01…F-173` IDs are the canonical product-portfolio identities; this blueprint's `FEAT-*` rows are a separate decomposition pending the one-to-many crosswalk in OPEN-41. Existing-project rule/state IDs such as legacy `BR-*` and `INVARIANT-001…` are **[REF]** only; proposed target rules use the independent `BR-T###` namespace. See the [legacy rule crosswalk](06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES.md#legacy-business-rule-crosswalk); numeric similarity does not imply equivalence.

### Consent scope IDs (CSC)

Consent scopes use dotted ids `<area>.<purpose>` (regex `^[a-z]+\.[a-z_]+(\.[a-z_]+)?$`, the third segment used only by `ai.assist.process`; for example `evidence.share_artefact`). The only list of valid ids, their grain, defaults, expiry, revocation effects and the old-to-canonical crosswalk is [CONSENT_SCOPE_CATALOGUE](#consent-scope-catalogue-csc) (14 scopes; enforced by FF-12). Legacy `CS-*` ids (`CS-DISCOVER` etc.) and `evidence.share_recruiter` are retired and must not be used in new text. Related families: `PUR-##` (processing purposes, PRIVACY design section 2; PUR-21..23 added for the split scopes), `OPEN-CS-##` (open items of the catalogue).

### Preservation evidence IDs

`DOC-000001` onward identifies source inventory rows in `KNOWLEDGE_PRESERVATION_MANIFEST.csv`. `EVD-000001` through `EVD-000204` are the source-document evidence pointers in that inventory. `EVD-000205` through `EVD-000242` are row-level evidence pointers in `28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/OBSERVED_IMPLEMENTATION_CONTROL_CROSSWALK.csv`. Each row retains its source DOC/EVD pointer, path, revision, hash and anchor. Assign subsequent IDs from the next unused number; never recycle an ID. The `TRC-*` document IDs identify traceability artifacts; TRC-07 is the observed implementation control crosswalk. `EVD-000243` through `EVD-000490` link the 248 historical business-rule audit records in `docs/reports/AUDIT_EVIDENCE_2026-10-21.md` to the corresponding `BR-*` rows in `06_BUSINESS_PROCESSES/LEGACY_RULE_CROSSWALK.csv`; full excerpts remain in the source JSON. `EVD-000491` through `EVD-000518` link the 28 historical audit journey records to source-registry lines and candidate `UJ-*` rows; the mapping remains unapproved under OPEN-50. `TRC-08` identifies this candidate journey evidence map. `EVD-000519` through `EVD-000796` provide row-level source pointers for the 278 routes compared with the target API catalog in `13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/OBSERVED_ROUTE_TARGET_CROSSWALK.csv`; the map is path/method-level only and remains under OPEN-46/50. `TRC-09` identifies the candidate route crosswalk. `EVD-000797` through `EVD-000847` point to the 51 migration records in the 2026-10-21 audit JSON; hashes match the checkout, while applied state remains unknown. `TRC-10` identifies the historical migration evidence crosswalk. `EVD-000848` through `EVD-002019` point to the 1,172 historical audit test assertions; statuses are dated memory-mode claims, and feature IDs are candidates only. `TRC-11` identifies the historical test evidence crosswalk. `EVD-002020` through `EVD-002021` point to the two historical synthetic probe records; they are not verification passes. `TRC-12` identifies the probe crosswalk. `EVD-002022` through `EVD-002054` point to 9 PR snapshots, 7 issue snapshots, and 17 issue comments in the audit JSON; external states remain historical and current status was not checked. `TRC-13` identifies the external issue crosswalk. `EVD-002055` through `EVD-002328` point to the 274 historical audit route records joined by exact method/path to the observed reference API inventory; the join is not a semantic contract match. `TRC-14` identifies the audit route evidence crosswalk. `EVD-002329` through `EVD-003012` point to the 684 historical audit inventory rows with current path/hash comparison; source coverage says inventoried, not necessarily inspected. No content-level disposition is implied. `TRC-15` identifies the audit inventory crosswalk. `EVD-003013` through `EVD-003058` map the 46 historical documentation cross-check rows to manifest DOC IDs; EVD-003059 through EVD-003060 retain the two absent deliverable-path claims. `TRC-16` identifies the audit documentation cross-check map.

EVD-003061 through EVD-003072 map 12 major claim groups from the 2026-10-22 architecture durability report DOC-000158 to target blueprint locations and later evidence, preserving historical revision, contradictions and validation limits. TRC-17 identifies this candidate crosswalk; source findings are not promoted as current defects or approved target requirements.

EVD-003073 through EVD-003082 map 10 major claim groups from source ADR-015 (DOC-000133) to target ADR-T05, data/migration design and later evidence. TRC-18 identifies this candidate crosswalk; OPEN-52 records the unresolved persistence bridge and owner/evidence gate.
