﻿# Knowledge Preservation Manifest

**Status: inventory established; source processing and cleanup are incomplete.** This register is the controlling record for legacy-source review. The companion CSV has one row per inventoried source and stable `DOC-*` / `EVD-*` identifiers. Do not treat inventory presence or provisional path-based classification as evidence that a source was read, migrated, or is eligible for deletion.

### Scope and baseline

- Repository root: `C:/Users/yashs/OneDrive/Desktop/talentsphere-complete`.
- Protected destination: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/`. Its 112 files present before this turn were preserved; the new source manifest CSV was added after the baseline enumeration and is included in the baseline CSV. The baseline CSV excludes itself and is separately required to remain present.
- External source inventory: 190 Markdown/text documents plus `docs/reports/AUDIT_EVIDENCE_2026-10-21.md` and 13 related non-text utility artifacts (204 total source records). The added non-text entries are protected/out of scope and remain uninspected. The JSON audit evidence is structured source material, not a disposable generated cache. The 28 historical journey records are also crosswalked to target UJ candidates (22 partial, 6 without a direct target journey); the map is unapproved and the manifest record remains partially processed.
- SSOT inventory: one accessible `SSOT.md`, at repository root. SHA-256 at inventory start: `891CB4A472B7BAA7C9259778922AE26AC46D8BDD97508B2B05C9FD0A2792F68D`. It is protected from modification, rename, move, and deletion.
- No external source has been deleted. No source is marked eligible for cleanup. Some entries are explicitly ineligible or out of scope; every unreviewed or incompletely mapped entry remains unresolved and must be preserved.
- `graphify-out/`, `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.github/`, `.playwright-*`, `.reticle/`, `.omc/`, application, test, deployment, and build/configuration artifacts are not included as source-document cleanup candidates merely because some are text or JSON. Their exclusions are recorded here as generated evidence, tool instructions/state, or protected technical artifacts; none were changed.

### Processing status vocabulary

Use these exact states in the CSV as review advances: `Read and fully processed`, `Read but partially processed`, `Not yet inspected`, `Unreadable`, `Out of scope`, `Preserved without migration`, `Migrated and verified`, `Retained as historical evidence`, `Eligible for cleanup`, `Deleted after verification`, `Protected from deletion`, and `Unresolved`. A source can carry a processing status plus a disposition; do not mark it migrated or eligible until destinations and evidence are recorded.

`SourceClassification` is provisional until content review. `TBD`, `Not yet assessed`, and `Not evaluated; do not delete` are deliberate unresolved values, not omissions. Assign each verified destination as a repository-relative path and cite the exact headings, IDs, rows, tests, or source evidence used. Record conflicts in the relevant open-decision, risk, ADR, or historical record before marking preservation complete.

- [Machine-readable source manifest](#knowledge_preservation_manifestcsv)
- [Protected destination tree baseline](#protected_document_tree_baselinecsv)

### Processing snapshot (2026-10-25)

The current 204-row manifest records 174 sources as `Read but partially processed`, 28 as `Out of scope`, 1 as `Read and fully processed`, and 1 as `Retained as historical evidence`. No source is eligible for cleanup. The high partial count reflects unfinished destination mapping, semantic review, evidence validation, or source-level disposition; it must not be read as 174 completed migrations. Recompute these totals from the CSV whenever statuses change.

### Initial findings

The documentation tree includes canonical intent (`SSOT.md`), product and engineering docs, route/security/test/operations material, dated audits, feature status and plan matrices, conflict/gap registers, and agent/tool-produced records. They have different authority and retention needs. Dated audit findings are historical evidence and cannot be silently rewritten as current requirements. Tool instructions/configuration and generated outputs are not automatic cleanup targets. The initial content review confirmed conflicts around target versus implemented architecture, phase vocabularies, privacy thresholds, pricing, ADR-015, and role models; preserve these as open conflicts until source-level mapping and the relevant owner decision are complete.

### Cleanup gate

No file may be deleted based on a filename, age, duplicate topic, or directory-level judgment. For each candidate, the CSV must first record source content reviewed, unique information, destination path(s), supporting evidence, contradictions/history, non-documentation dependency checks, and a final rationale. Any unknown, unreadable, unverified, or unmapped source remains preserved. Deletions, if later made, must be individual and followed by checks that every `SSOT.md` and every baseline file still exists.

### Read-only source review: `docs/` and root `SSOT.md`

A read-only subagent reviewed the 49 files under `docs/` (48 Markdown and one JSON) plus the root `SSOT.md`. Individual read scope is recorded in the review findings; partial means sections or structure were sampled, and the largest feature matrices and JSON evidence ledger were not read in full. The CSV marks these records `Read but partially processed` because migration and destination verification are still pending, including for sources fully read.

- `docs/product/PRD.md` → candidate destinations in product vision, capability map, feature specs, and success metrics. It covers the north-star loop, personas/JTBD, non-goals, launch scope, and measures. Feature-register authority differs between PRD and SSOT/registry.

### Documentation consolidation — 2026-10-27

Scope: 23 tracked Markdown revision/addendum records within this blueprint. The user's current request authorizes reducing document count while preserving useful knowledge; GPT independent review was explicitly authorized in this chat. This scoped authorization supersedes the earlier additive-only/folder-retention rule for these 23 records only. SSOT, agent/tool instructions, application code, build files, machine-readable contracts, historical inventory CSVs, untracked graph output and pre-existing user edits remain outside this cleanup.

Each complete source record is incorporated into its existing owning document, with an explicit source boundary, original title, full wording, stable record/section anchors and repaired references. No requirement, example, exception, proposal, approval condition, command, diagram, table or rationale is intentionally omitted. Source-local numbering remains local to its labelled record. REV2 records retain their prior approval status; APIC-02 sections 3–5 remain applicable overrides. Older dated counts and statements about files are historical observations, not fresh completion claims. This is structural consolidation, not semantic deduplication or product reapproval.

SSOT.md receives reference-only repairs in Appendix F; its product requirements and approval status remain unchanged. No root or tool-required document is removed.

Recovery revision: `cf8b3657d8747ab934c45a79a66570be3365cf37`. Every selected source was clean and matched the committed text before consolidation; source-byte SHA-256 values are below. Every original heading maps to the same record anchor with `--<original-heading-slug>` appended; every paragraph/table/code block between headings stays within that section. This maps all source text, not only named requirement IDs. Source stems (including `BACKEND_ARCHITECTURE_REV2` in the historical OpenAPI description) remain searchable aliases in the preserved record titles; the OpenAPI description is provenance, not a file-loading dependency. Historical inventory/baseline paths retain their historical meaning and are resolved through this table.

Validation scope: exact complete-record comparison after deterministic reference rewriting and anchor insertion; every source-heading anchor; baseline-versus-current Markdown file/fragment link checks; tracked reference search including non-Markdown artifacts; Git recovery comparison; non-document/protected-file hashes; working-tree and whitespace checks. Existing broken links to absent legacy material are reported separately rather than counted as new regressions. Reticle and application builds/tests are skipped because this changes documentation only. No security policy, database schema, entitlement, application behavior or production system changes; no migrations. No fresh product-completeness or production-readiness claim is made.

Executed results: 23 complete records and 189 source-heading anchors passed; 924 relative Markdown links inspected, 82 pointing to consolidated records; 0 new broken links and 0 stale source links. The scanner reports 37 pre-existing broken-link occurrences before and 37 after; this is a bounded inline-Markdown-link scan, not validation of remote URLs or every Markdown extension. Protected/non-document hash mismatches: 0.

### 4. Conflicts found (both sides kept; decision ids in `OPEN_DECISIONS_LEGACY_MERGE.md`)

1. Legacy business rules vs target BR-T: 8 conflict rows (BR-015, 036, 057, 095, 100, 128, 130, 190) and 1 superseded (BR-035); 4 legacy-vs-SSOT text differences (BR-065, 066, 158, 178). Decisions: OPEN-43, OPEN-09, OPEN-33, OPEN-LGA-03, OPEN-LGA-04.
2. Eleven tensions inside the legacy rule corpus (duplicates, k thresholds, completion vs final exam, billing audience, caps, decay, promotion). OPEN-LGA-01, OPEN-LGA-02, OPEN-LGA-06.
3. Legacy feature registry: 24 phase-order inversions and one dependency cycle (F-45 and F-115). OPEN-LGA-05.
4. Pricing: legacy concrete plan prices vs target "no prices proposed"; tier names differ. OPEN-19, OPEN-LGA-17, OPEN-LGA-18.
5. Design system: motion tokens 120/200/320 ms vs 80/150/240/400 ms; state vocabularies; "Aura" token origin. OPEN-LGA-16.
6. Strategy: legacy 13-stage loop, 6 goals, extra non-goals and GA path vs STR-01; primary metric (existing OPEN-51). OPEN-LGA-09, OPEN-LGA-10.
7. Journeys and roles: 6 legacy journeys without target match; persona and role counts (14, 19, 3+24, 10) vs target. OPEN-45, OPEN-44, OPEN-LGA-07, OPEN-LGA-08.
8. Prior-attempt stack and ADR numbering vs target ADRs (C-5, C-13, C-14). OPEN-LGA-20, OPEN-LGA-21, OPEN-52.
9. Internal conflicts inside MEMORY.md (migration, feature-maturity and test counts) preserved as history.

Total new open decisions: 21 (OPEN-LGA-01 to OPEN-LGA-21).

### 5. Unread, absent or out-of-group material

- `docs/superpowers/**`: absent on this branch.
- Documents the legacy files reference but that are outside group A and were not read here: `docs/engineering/*`, `docs/quality/*`, `docs/reports/*`, `docs/system-model/*` (other merge groups); the retired `WORKFLOWS_AND_BUSINESS_RULES.md`, `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`, `SOURCE_RECONCILIATION.md` (their content is described in `30_PRIOR_IMPLEMENTATION_REFERENCE/08-ROADMAP-AND-DECISIONS.md` Appendix A/B); the root `SSOT.md` (protected; canonical wording of BR-065/066/158/178 and persona definitions P-A to P-T live there).
- Group A items that depend on those documents are flagged as open (OPEN-LGA-07, OPEN-LGA-15, OPEN-LGA-19).

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-b--1-scope-and-method"></a>

### 1. Scope and method

- Branch: `chore/remove-app-code-keep-docs-2026-10-26` only. No other branch, no `git show main:`.
- Frame: no source code exists; **nothing is implemented**. Legacy statements are rewritten as "prior attempt reported X" or "target requires X". Verbatim legacy blocks are always introduced by a frame paragraph that says they describe an earlier attempt.
- Source group B: `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE/docs/engineering/**` (including `adr/`), `.../docs/system-model/**`, `.../docs/quality/**`, `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE/deploy/*.md`, plus root `ARCHITECTURE.md` (read, not edited). 22 archive files plus the root file. No source file was edited, moved or deleted.
- Method: each source was read in full. Narrative was rewritten by hand into the target folder that matches its subject. Large tables, diagrams and ADR texts were copied **unchanged** by a script (line-range includes) so no character was retyped; the script only removed legacy relative markdown links (their targets do not exist in this tree) and removed the duplicate title line. Source files contain `?` where non-ASCII characters were lost in an earlier export; copied blocks keep the `?`.
- Folders edited (ownership list): 08, 09, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18, 23, 25 (new files only), plus one appended index line in each of `11_BACKEND_ARCHITECTURE/INDEX.md`, `12_DATA_AND_DATABASE/INDEX.md`, `13_API_EVENTS_AND_INTEGRATIONS/INDEX.md`, `14_SECURITY_PRIVACY_COMPLIANCE/INDEX.md`, and this ledger in 00. Not touched: `SSOT.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.env` files, `MASTER_INDEX*`, `24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS/*` (existing OPEN ids are cited, never renumbered; new items are local ids in the new files), `28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/*`, `CANONICAL_INVARIANTS.md`.

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-b--4-conflicts-and-decisions-surfaced-full-index-23_migration_and_evolutionlegacy_merge_conflict_registermd-section-2"></a>

### 4. Conflicts and decisions surfaced (full index: `23_MIGRATION_AND_EVOLUTION/LEGACY_MERGE_CONFLICT_REGISTER.md` section 2)

- 53 local items (ids `L-B08-..` to `L-B25-..`); about 20 are true legacy-versus-target conflicts, the rest are gap checks or ownership questions. Existing OPEN ids reused, none renumbered: OPEN-08, 12, 14, 17, 19, 22, 25, 26, 27, 33, 40, 41, 42, 43, 44, 46, 47, 48, 49, 50, 52.
- Highest-impact items:
  - **ADR-015 hybrid read model versus ADR-T05 SQL-only** (OPEN-52): kept visible; the legacy ADR is a lesson, not a decision.
  - **Three ADR numbering namespaces** (legacy SSOT-aligned ADR-001..013, implemented-architecture ADR-001..011/015/016, blueprint ADR-T01..T23) collide on ADR-001 (L-B11-03).
  - **India / DPDP absent from the target compliance matrix** (L-B14-03): text search of the whole target blueprint finds no mention; the legacy security doc told the program to map to the DPDP Act and the prior hosting was in Mumbai. Decision needed from Legal/Privacy (OPEN-08, 30, 34).
  - **Test retries**: legacy zero retries and `forbidOnly` versus target one retry for E2E (L-B16-01).
  - **Password hashing** PBKDF2 versus argon2id (L-B14-01), **bearer storage versus cookies** (L-B14-02), **400 versus 422 and 409 versus 422** (L-B13-01).
  - **Employer-domain verification rule** (legacy CF-11, still undecided: L-B14-05), **referee decline/flag** (CF-22: L-B23-01), **reference-link validity** 30 versus 14 days (L-B15-03), **supersession and dispute policy** for payments (L-B13-02), **erasure grace period** (L-B12-01).
  - Legacy INVARIANT-002, 003, 004, 005, 009, 011 have no INV-T counterpart (L-B11-04); `CANONICAL_INVARIANTS.md` was not edited.
- Corrections to earlier assumptions made during the merge: the client server-state library is already decided in the blueprint (FD-F2 TanStack Query v5), so the legacy TRD row is consistent; the legacy count of "16 threats" is 15 in the actual list.

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-b--5-verification-performed"></a>

### 5. Verification performed

- Endpoint index: all 278 legacy rows compared with `13_*/OBSERVED_REFERENCE_API_INVENTORY.csv` by method+path, registry flag and source anchor: 278 of 278 present, 0 differing.
- Line-range includes: every range was checked against the source for table or fence boundaries (header and separator present, no cut rows); Mermaid code fences in the diagram library file are balanced (12 `mermaid` blocks, 24 fences).
- Secret scan of all new files (live-key prefixes, webhook secret prefixes, JWT-like strings, credentialed connection strings): none found. The only connection string is a documented placeholder (`REPLACE_ME`); the hosted project reference is an identifier, not a credential.
- Existing ids used in cross-references (ADR-T01..T23, INV-T01..T20, DOM-01..24, OPEN-nn, Doc IDs of target files) were read from the target files before use. Cross-references to RB/FM/ALT/TEST ids were kept generic where the exact id was not confirmed.
- Not performed: Mermaid syntax validation (no tool run), link checking of the new files, rendering. No application code, tests or build were run (none exist).

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-c--legacy-merge-ledger-c-preservation-record-for-source-group-c-dated-reports"></a>

## Legacy Merge Ledger C (preservation record for source group C: dated reports)

Doc ID: CC-LEDGER-C · Owner: Documentation Architect · Version 1.0 · Status: Draft for Review · Date: 2026-10-26 · Relates: `LEGACY_MERGE_LEDGER_A.md`, `KNOWLEDGE_PRESERVATION_MANIFEST.md`, `INFORMATION_RECONCILIATION_MATRIX.md`, `MASTER_INDEX.md`

Supersedes nothing. This ledger is the link target for the four `LEGACY_*` and `*_FROM_PRIOR_ATTEMPT` files created by the Group C merge. `MASTER_INDEX.md` is not edited by Group C and must link here and to the four files (control-centre owner action). Created under the standing instruction: "i dont want to loos information keep everything in use untile it get improved".

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-c--1-scope-and-method"></a>

### 1. Scope and method

- Branch: `chore/remove-app-code-keep-docs-2026-10-26` only. No other branch, no `git show main:`.
- Frame: no source code exists; **nothing is implemented**. Legacy statements are rewritten as "prior attempt reported X; target must prevent X" or as acceptance criteria the target must meet. No sentence in the new files presents a legacy capability as present.
- Source group C: `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE/docs/reports/**` (16 files: 15 Markdown, 1 JSON). Source files are untouched: no deletion, no edit, no move.
- All 16 files were read. The 144 KB feature-status report, the 154 KB feature plan and the 63 KB traceability matrix were read in portions (offset and limit). The 1.83 MB evidence JSON was read structurally by script (top-level keys, record counts, status distributions, field names, samples) and its 173-feature, 46-contract and 155-intent-sentence content was extracted by script. Repetitive per-feature table rows (the backlog and build-order rows of the plan) were checked by script for equality with the JSON (169 backlog rows and 153 build-order rows matched on dependencies and acceptance with zero differences; two further backlog rows are story rows); the 173 feature-level rows were read.
- Large generated tables (173-feature register, 46 contracts, 155 intent sentences, 173-row module and table map, 115-file test inventory) were produced by script from the archived sources and checked by row count (173, 46, 155 plus 18 placeholders, 173, 115 files with 1,172 tests). Narrative sections were written by hand and checked against the sources.
- Not edited: `SSOT.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.env` files, `MASTER_INDEX*`, any existing file. No secrets were read or written (the reports contain none; hosted project ids and GitHub comment ids are public identifiers).
- New files by Group C (4 plus this ledger):

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-c--3-information-deliberately-kept-only-in-the-archive"></a>

### 3. Information deliberately kept only in the archive

These items were judged to be implementation description of the earlier code, machine evidence, or evidence of artifacts that no longer exist; they remain unmodified in `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE/docs/reports/` and are cited by file and ID.

- Per-feature actual-behavior prose, UI and backend paths, and endpoint route lists (status and traceability reports; JSON).
- Row-level machine evidence in the JSON (rules, routes, migrations, file hashes, test names, issue-comment bodies).
- Maintenance reports' size tables and per-test timings.
- SQL and code snippets, source line numbers, exact exploit mechanics beyond the stated remedy.
- External hyperlinks to the earlier GitHub repository, CI runs and issues (ids are recorded where useful).

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-c--4-cross-links-added-by-this-merge"></a>

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-c--5-items-for-the-control-centre-owner-not-done-by-group-c"></a>

### 5. Items for the control-centre owner (not done by Group C)

1. Link this ledger and the four files from `MASTER_INDEX.md` (an addendum is the established pattern) and add the four Doc IDs (`RSK-LESSONS-C`, `SPEC-LEGACY-C`, `RDM-GATES-C`, `TRC-LEGACY-C`) and the new prefixes (`LES-`, `LGR-`, `LRG-`, `LEI-`) to `ID_REGISTRY.md`.
2. Decide whether the 88 lessons should be merged into `RISK_REGISTER` rows (the lessons file is an input register; it does not assign likelihood or owners).
3. Reconcile the two issue-number mappings (lessons section 12) if the earlier GitHub issues are ever consulted again; prefer the mapping read from issue bodies.
4. Confirm that `OPEN_DECISIONS.md` covers the owner decisions listed in lessons section 13 (architecture of record, commercial scope, email provider, legal review, erasure semantics, scoring rule, session storage, sandbox technology, password policy, maintenance-mode exemptions).
5. The evidence JSON's feature-level `previousAssessment`, `maturity` and `canonicalContract` fields are queryable by script; if the target adopts a coverage tool, ingest from the archived JSON instead of re-typing.

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-d--legacy-merge-ledger-d-gap-closure-after-the-independent-preservation-audit"></a>

## Legacy Merge Ledger D (gap closure after the independent preservation audit)

Doc ID: CC-LEDGER-D · Owner: Documentation Architect · Version 1.0 · Status: Draft for Review · Date: 2026-10-27 · Supersedes nothing · Relates: [`LEGACY_MERGE_LEDGER_A.md`](#), [`LEGACY_MERGE_LEDGER_B.md`](#record-legacy-merge-ledger-b), [`LEGACY_MERGE_LEDGER_C.md`](#record-legacy-merge-ledger-c), [`KNOWLEDGE_PRESERVATION_MANIFEST.md`](#knowledge_preservation_manifestmd) · Linked from: [`MASTER_INDEX.md#record-master-index-v9-addendum-3`](#record-master-index-v9-addendum-3)

Created under the standing instruction: "i dont want to loos information keep everything in use untile it get improved".

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-d--1-scope-and-method"></a>

### 1. Scope and method

- Branch: `chore/remove-app-code-keep-docs-2026-10-26` only. No other branch, no `git show main:`.
- Frame: nothing is implemented. Every closure section is headed "Merged from legacy: <source path>" and states that the prior attempt reported or required the quoted items; quoted code paths, hashes and counts are evidence about the prior attempt only.
- Input: the independent validator's preservation report (50 archive files: FULL 26, PARTIAL 24, NOT 0; 36 missing named ids; 8 partial JSON members).
- Method: for each PARTIAL source, the named sentences and tables were quoted by line range from the unchanged archive file by script (no retyping), and every source line carrying a backticked code token, id, date, commit hash, migration name or URL that was absent from the blueprint was quoted as well. Quotes are line-numbered (`L<n>`) in fenced blocks so legacy relative links do not become broken links. Each block is appended at the end of the existing target file (no existing line was edited). Two CSV files and one quote file are new.
- Not touched: `SSOT.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.env` files, any archive file. Nothing deleted or moved. Archive files stay in use.
- New files: this ledger; [`LEGACY_TRACEABILITY_MATRIX_ROWS_FROM_PRIOR_ATTEMPT.md`](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART4.md#legacy-feature-traceability-matrix-rows-verbatim-quote-from-the-prior-attempt); [`LEGACY_AUDIT_JSON_PER_FEATURE_JUDGEMENTS.csv`](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART4.md#legacy_audit_json_per_feature_judgementscsv); [`LEGACY_AUDIT_JSON_PER_BUSINESS_RULE_JUDGEMENTS.csv`](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART4.md#legacy_audit_json_per_business_rule_judgementscsv).

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-d--4-verification-re-run-2026-10-27-script-in-the-session-scratch-folder-repository-untouched-by-it"></a>

### 4. Verification (re-run 2026-10-27, script in the session scratch folder, repository untouched by it)

Method: extract every `XX-nn` id from the 49 archived Markdown files and search the blueprint (all folders except `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE`, including the new files); extract every backticked code token, id, date, commit hash, migration name and URL from each source line of the 23 Markdown PARTIAL files and search the blueprint; check every string of the six small JSON members.

| Check                                                                                                                                     | Before (audit)                                     | After                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| Distinct source ids missing from the blueprint                                                                                            | 36 of 532                                          | **0 of 532**                   |
| Named ids (QW-03, QW-05, QW-06, TD-04, CWE-328, CWE-613, CWE-916, BL-032, TC-01..TC-38) missing                                           | 36                                                 | **0**                          |
| Code/id/date/hash/migration/URL tokens missing in the 23 Markdown PARTIAL files                                                           | several hundred (about 1,000 by the audit's count) | **0**                          |
| JSON members snapshot, method, inspectionLimitations, contractInventory, artifactValidation, focusedContractReviews: leaf strings missing | 8 members partial                                  | **0** (all leaf strings found) |

<a id="record-legacy-merge-ledger-d--5-remaining-gaps-honest-count"></a>

### 5. Remaining gaps (honest count)

- **Missing ids: 0. Missing named code/URL/hash tokens: 0.**
- JSON `features` and `businessRules`: **2,701 leaf strings** are still only in the archived JSON (1,698 in `features`, 1,003 in `businessRules`). They are prior-code reference excerpts and line numbers (`referenceCandidates`, `references[].excerpt`), `testEvidence.association` text, and `subfeatures`/`canonicalContract` text that derives from the retained `SSOT.md`. The per-row judgements (`nextAction`, `previousAssessment`, all `maturity` dimensions, rule `nextAction`) are now exported to the two CSV files. The JSON must stay archived; recovery commands are in `28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/LEGACY_REPORTS_EVIDENCE_INDEX.md` section D-24.
- Table-row condensation in the large reports (the audit counted, for example, 397 of 416 rows in `FEATURE_PLAN`, 268 of 434 in `FEATURE_STATUS`, 167 of 268 in `REPOSITORY_AUDIT`, 108 of 111 in `MAINTENANCE_AUDIT`): every id, code token, number, hash and URL in those rows is now present, but sentence-level wording of reworded rows was **not re-measured** by a new sentence-coverage run. The source text remains in the archive. Status for those files: closed at token and named-item level, not proven at sentence level.
- Link-only and prose wording differences judged immaterial by the audit were not chased (for example `docs/INDEX.md` relative link targets are quoted inside fenced blocks, not as live links).
- Deletion readiness is unchanged: no archive file is cleared for deletion. All 50 archive files stay in use.
- Not done by this ledger: Mermaid or link checking, rendering of the appended blocks, update of `KNOWLEDGE_PRESERVATION_MANIFEST.csv` rows.

### Completeness addendum (2026-10-27): every previously non-indexed file, plus precedence rules

Source of the list: a read-only link scan on 2026-10-27 found 115 files not reachable from this index (112 at the earlier scan plus 3 later additions). 27 of them are tool-state files under `.omc/` paths and one is a stray log (`.reticle-setup-crash.log`, repository tool output, path only); these are **not documents**, are deliberately not linked, and are not deleted. The remaining 87 files are linked below, grouped by folder. Linking is not approval: every `*_REV2`, `*_REVIEW_FIXES`, JSON/YAML and CSV file keeps the status given in `DOCUMENT_STATUS.md`.

Related additions in this revision (all in place, nothing moved): [ID families](#id-registry-canonical--all-documents-must-use-these-ids) · [OPEN_DECISIONS section 6](24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md#open-decisions-register) · [G01..G16 ledger](22_IMPLEMENTATION_ROADMAP.md#implementation-roadmap) · [prompt coverage matrix](28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE_PART3.md#coverage-matrices-ac-to-test-br-to-ac-to-test-per-feature-ac-counts) · [50 acceptance questions](99_FINAL_MASTER_BLUEPRINT.md#talentsphere--project-master-blueprint-target-design) · [manifest Part 66.3](#knowledge_preservation_manifestmd) · [cold-start rehearsal](29_DEVELOPER_IMPLEMENTATION_GUIDE.md#developer-implementation-guide) · [link triage and evidence labels](#document-status-register).

#### V9 index addenda (read in this order)

[MASTER_INDEX_V9_ADDENDUM](#record-master-index-v9-addendum) (control documents, review artifacts) → [MASTER_INDEX_V9_ADDENDUM_2](#record-master-index-v9-addendum-2) (review-fix `*_REV2` overlays) → [MASTER_INDEX_V9_ADDENDUM_3](#record-master-index-v9-addendum-3) (second review; ledger D closure). The three are additive companions of this file; this file stays the entry point.

#### Precedence rules (no file deleted)

1. **Originals versus `*_REV2` files.** On 2026-10-27 the REV2 content was folded into the originals (see each original's "Revision 2 folded in" banner). Where an original and its REV2 file differ, the original governs. REV2 files are change records and are kept. Example: [ROADMAP](22_IMPLEMENTATION_ROADMAP.md#implementation-roadmap) governs over [ROADMAP_REV2](22_IMPLEMENTATION_ROADMAP.md#record-roadmap-rev2); [RISK_REGISTER](24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md#risk-register) over [RISK_REGISTER_REV2](24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md#record-risk-register-rev2). Exception: where an original has not been folded yet, the REV2 text is a PROPOSED amendment and the original still governs until an owner ratifies.
2. **`30_PRIOR_IMPLEMENTATION_REFERENCE` versus `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE`.** Both are reference only and neither is target truth. `31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE` holds unmodified source evidence (dated, commit-bound) and wins on what the prior attempt recorded at that date; `30_PRIOR_IMPLEMENTATION_REFERENCE` is an analysis of it and wins on interpretation and reading order, but must not contradict the archive's facts. If they disagree, cite the archive and record the difference as an OPEN item. Target decisions always live in folders 01 to 29 and `24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS`.
3. **Root `SSOT.md`** stays protected founder intent; the blueprint and ADRs record where the target differs.

## 31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE

The legacy Markdown documents that used to be archived here (`docs/`, `BRAIN/`, `deploy/*.md`) were merged into the final blueprint (see `00_CONTROL_CENTRE/LEGACY_MERGE_LEDGER_A.md` to `_D.md`) and removed on 2026-10-27. Every file is recoverable from git commit `85706db0`:

```bash
git show 85706db0:Yash_Final_Project_Document/31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE/<path>
```

Kept here: `docs/reports/AUDIT_EVIDENCE_2026-10-21.md`, the structured audit evidence of the earlier attempt (indexed in `28_TRACEABILITY_AND_COVERAGE/LEGACY_REPORTS_EVIDENCE_INDEX.md`); about 2,700 detail strings exist only there.
<!-- source-record-end: Yash_Final_Project_Document/31_LEGACY_ARCHIVE/README.md -->

## Project Scope & Execution Boundary

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/PROJECT_SCOPE.md`._

Doc ID: CC-SCOPE · Version 1.0 · Status: Final for this initiative

**Mission:** design and document the strongest practical target version of TalentSphere from first principles, as an implementation-ready blueprint.

**Allowed:** read repo files for context; research; write documentation under `Yash_Final_Project_Document/` only.
**Not done / not allowed:** application source changes, migrations, deployments, infra, secrets, pushes/merges, commits (no commit made).
**Model policy:** orchestrator = Claude Sonnet 5.5; all delegated agents invoked with `model: sonnet`. Haiku was not used. Runtime enforcement of the model setting is by the Agent tool parameter; it was not independently verified beyond that parameter.
**Authority of inputs:** existing `SSOT.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, and `docs/` are preserved **[REF]** evidence, not automatically adopted target requirements. The blueprint is a proposed target; source-to-target differences remain unresolved until recorded with owner disposition. Target decisions live in `24_RISKS_AND_DECISIONS/ADR_REGISTER.md` and open owner decisions in `OPEN_DECISIONS.md`.
**Reality check:** this is a design; no feature is claimed implemented, and documentation completeness does not prove production readiness.
**Honest limits of this initiative:** no real stakeholder research; platform (iOS/Android) behaviour from secondary sources, unverified on devices; no legal review; no pricing, cost or schedule data; Module Federation + Vite plugin stability needs a spike (OPEN-11).

## Source Document Inventory (V9 control document)

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/SOURCE_DOCUMENT_INVENTORY.md`._

Doc ID: CC-SRC · Version 1.0 · Status: Draft · Added additively; no existing file was changed to create it.

The controlling inventory is already maintained as the machine-readable [KNOWLEDGE_PRESERVATION_MANIFEST.csv](#knowledge_preservation_manifestcsv) with narrative in [KNOWLEDGE_PRESERVATION_MANIFEST.md](#knowledge_preservation_manifestmd) and a protected-tree baseline in [PROTECTED_DOCUMENT_TREE_BASELINE.csv](#protected_document_tree_baselinecsv). This file does not duplicate rows; it records the V9 checks and where each V9 inventory field lives.

### Verified observations (2026-10-25, this session, read-only commands)

- Repository root: `C:/Users/yashs/OneDrive/Desktop/talentsphere-complete`.
- `SSOT.md` files found: exactly one (`./SSOT.md`, repo root, excluding `node_modules`). `git status --porcelain -- SSOT.md` returned nothing (no uncommitted change).
- `git status --porcelain` outside `Yash_Final_Project_Document/` returned nothing: no uncommitted change to application source, config or external docs.
- Files in `Yash_Final_Project_Document/`: 158, all tracked by git at the time of the check.
- Markdown files outside the doc root (excluding node_modules, graphify-out, .omc): 187 found by `find`; the manifest records 204 source records (190 text docs, 1 JSON evidence file, 13 non-text utility artifacts).

## Change record: Execution Ledger — V9 Addendum (2026-10-25)

_Former file: `Yash_Final_Project_Document/00_CONTROL_CENTRE/EXECUTION_LEDGER_V9_ADDENDUM.md`._

Doc ID: CC-LED2 · Additive; the original [EXECUTION_LEDGER](#execution-ledger) is left as is (V9 forbids overwriting existing files in this root).

### Honest disclosure about preservation

The V8 phase of this work edited files **in place** inside this folder: the orchestrator's own earlier files and, through delegated Sonnet agents, many agent-written files (consent catalogue, state-machine vocabulary, cookie contract, audit/erasure design, permission-matrix rows, ledger append, status lines in the blueprint and readiness docs). These edits were made before the V9 rule ("existing files inside the root must not be destructively changed") was given. Git history holds the prior versions where those files were committed (the folder was committed by another session; check `git log -- Yash_Final_Project_Document`). From this point the work is **additive only** in this root: new revision/addendum files, no in-place edits.

### State at this checkpoint

- Preservation checks run now: one `SSOT.md`, unchanged per git; no uncommitted change outside the doc root; 158 files in the root.
- V9 control documents added: [SOURCE_DOCUMENT_INVENTORY](#source-document-inventory-v9-control-document-1), [INFORMATION_RECONCILIATION_MATRIX](#information-reconciliation-matrix-v9-control-document), [CLEANUP_CANDIDATES](#cleanup-candidate-register-v9-control-document) (0 eligible). The manifest and baseline were produced by a parallel effort and are reused, not duplicated.
- Review findings: REVW-01..05 resolved in docs. REVW-06..45: four fix agents (frontend/PWA/federation, product/rules/roadmap, backend/data/API/security, ops/QA/cost) were interrupted twice by usage limits (reset 6pm America/Los_Angeles); partial edits exist, completion unverified. Binding decisions are in `27_INDEPENDENT_REVIEWS/REVIEW_RESOLUTION_DECISIONS.md`.
- Final blueprint exists at `99_FINAL_MASTER_BLUEPRINT/PROJECT_MASTER_BLUEPRINT.md`; its status line says Revision Required. `99_*/FINAL_REVIEW_REPORT.md` (V9 layout) does not exist: the review lives at `27_INDEPENDENT_REVIEWS/FINAL_REVIEW_REPORT.md`.
- Model policy: Sonnet only, via the Agent tool `model` parameter; effort used: high/xhigh. Not independently verified beyond that parameter. Haiku not used.
- Validation not performed in this checkpoint: Redocly/AsyncAPI re-run after the interrupted edits, Mermaid rendering, link re-check after the latest parallel edits.

### Next actions (when limits reset)

1. Resume the four fix agents (SendMessage by id) in additive mode: write revisions as new files or addenda and link them from the index, per V9.
2. Re-run YAML/JSON/link/ID scans; update validation notes in a new addendum.
3. Governance pass: register ADR-T23+ and consolidate OPEN-FE/UX/B/CS ids in a new register file.
